Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any quantum_mechanics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 288. The projective resolution has length at most 35 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

This completes the proof. QED.
